           cam_arr = traj_raw.flat[cam_idx]\n   363→            cam_out = {'ts': [], 'frameTimes': [], 'featNames': []}\n   364→\n   365→            if cam_arr is None or (isinstance(cam_arr, np.ndarray) and cam_arr.size == 0):\n   366→                session['traj'].append(cam_out)\n   367→                continue\n   368→\n   369→            # cam_arr: structured array (1, nTrials) with fields per trial\n   370→            if hasattr(cam_arr, 'dtype') and cam_arr.dtype.names is not None:\n   371→                cam_flat = cam_arr.flatten()\n   372→\n   373→                # Feature names from first trial\n   374→                for t_check in range(min(len(cam_flat), n_trials)):\n   375→                    fn_field = cam_flat[t_check]['featNames']\n   376→                    if fn_field is not None and np.array(fn_field).size > 0:\n   377→                        fn_flat = np.array(fn_field).flatten()\n   378→                        names = [str(np.array(fn_flat[i]).flat[0]).strip()\n   379→                                if np.array(fn_flat[i]).size > 0 else ''\n   380→                                for i in range(len(fn_flat))]\n   381→                        cam_out['featNames'] = names\n   382→                        break\n   383→\n   384→                # Per-trial ts and frameTimes\n   385→                for t in range(min(len(cam_flat), n_trials)):\n   386→                    trial_struct = cam_flat[t]\n   387→\n   388→                    # ts: (nFrames, 3, nFeatures) -> transpose to (nFeatures, 3, nFrames)\n   389→                    ts_val = trial_struct['ts']\n   390→                    if isinstance(ts_val, np.ndarray) and ts_val.ndim == 3 and ts_val.size > 0:\n   391→                        cam_out['ts'].append(ts_val.transpose(2, 1, 0))\n   392→                    else:\n   393→                        cam_out['ts'].append(None)\n   394→\n   395→                    ft_val = trial_struct['frameTimes']\n   396→                    if isinstance(ft_val, np.ndarray) and ft_val.size > 0:\n   397→                        cam_out['frameTimes'].append(ft_val.flatten())\n   398→                    else:\n   399→                        cam_out['frameTimes'].append(None)\n   400→\n   401→                # Pad if fewer trials in traj\n   402→                while len(cam_out['ts']) < n_trials:\n   403→                    cam_out['ts'].append(None)\n   404→                    cam_out['frameTimes'].append(None)\n   405→\n   406→            session['traj'].append(cam_out)\n   407→\n   408→    # Video offset\n   409→    try:\n   410→        sglx = obj['sglx'][0, 0]\n   411→        bp_bitstart = np.array(ev['bitStart']).flatten()\n   412→        bc = sglx['bitcode'][0, 0]\n   413→        sglx_bitstart = np.array(bc['bitstart']).flatten()\n   414→        sglx_fs = float(np.array(sglx['fs']).flatten()[0])\n   415→        mode_bp = scipy_stats.mode(bp_bitstart[~np.isnan(bp_bitstart)], keepdims=False).mode\n   416→        mode_sglx = scipy_stats.mode(sglx_bitstart[~np.isnan(sglx_bitstart)], keepdims=False).mode\n   417→        session['vidshift'] = mode_sglx / sglx_fs - mode_bp\n   418→    except:\n   419→        session['vidshift'] = 0.5\n   420→\n   421→    # Motion energy (embedded in obj.me)\n   422→    if _has_field(obj, 'me'):\n   423→        me_raw = obj['me']\n   424→        me_data = []\n   425→        me_flat = me_raw.flatten()\n   426→        for t in range(n_trials):\n   427→            try:\n   428→                me_trial = np.array(me_flat[t]).flatten() if t < len(me_flat) else None\n   429→                me_data.append(me_trial)\n   430→            except:\n   431→                me_data.append(None)\n   432→        session['me_embedded'] = me_data\n   433→\n   434→    return session\n   435→\n   436→\n   437→def load_motion_energy_file(me_path):\n   438→    \"\"\"Load motion energy from separate .mat file.\"\"\"\n   439→    d = scipy.io.loadmat(me_path, simplify_cells=True)\n   440→    me = d['me']\n   441→\n   442→    # Format 1: struct with 'data' and 'moveThresh' fields\n   443→    if isinstance(me, dict) and 'data' in me:\n   444→        data = me['data']\n   445→        thresh = me.get('moveThresh', 10)\n   446→    elif isinstance(me, np.ndarray) and me.dtype == object:\n   447→        # Format 2: just an array of per-trial arrays (no struct wrapper)\n   448→        data = me\n   449→        thresh = 10  # default\n   450→    else:\n   451→        return [], 10.0\n   452→\n   453→    me_trials = []\n   454→    if isinstance(data, list):\n   455→        for trial_data in data:\n   456→            me_trials.append(np.array(trial_data).flatten() if trial_data is not None else None)\n   457→    elif isinstance(data, np.ndarray):\n   458→        if data.dtype == object:\n   459→            for trial_data in data.flatten():\n   460→                me_trials.append(np.array(trial_data).flatten() if trial_data is not None else None)\n   461→        else:\n   462→            me_trials.append(data.flatten())\n   463→\n   464→    return me_trials, float(thresh)\n   465→\n   466→\n   467→def load_session(fpath):\n   468→    \"\"\"Load session from either h5py or scipy format.\"\"\"\n   469→    try:\n   470→        session = load_session_h5(fpath)\n   471→        return session\n   472→    except:\n   473→        pass\n   474→    try:\n   475→        session = load_session_scipy(fpath)\n   476→        return session\n   477→    except Exception as e:\n   478→        print(f\"  ERROR loading {fpath}: {e}\")\n   479→        return None\n   480→\n   481→\n   482→# ============================================================\n   483→# Processing functions\n   484→# ============================================================\n   485→def align_and_bin_spikes(neurons, goCue, n_trials, edges, dt):\n   486→    \"\"\"Align spikes to go cue and bin into time bins.\n   487→\n   488→    Returns: (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_trials) array of firing rates in Hz.\n   489→    \"\"\"\n   490→    n_timebins = len(edges) - 1\n   491→    n_neurons_total = len(neurons)\n   492→\n   493→    # Pre-allocate\n   494→    trialdat = np.zeros((n_neurons_total, n_timebins, n_trials), dtype=np.float32)\n   495→\n   496→    for ni, neuron in enumerate(neurons):\n   497→        trialtm = neuron['trialtm']\n   498→        trial = neuron['trial']\n   499→\n   500→        for t in range(n_trials):\n   501→            trial_num = t + 1  # MATLAB 1-indexed\n   502→            gc = goCue[t]\n   503→            if np.isnan(gc):\n   504→                continue\n   505→\n   506→            # Get spikes for this trial\n   507→            spike_mask = trial == trial_num\n   508→            if not np.any(spike_mask):\n   509→                continue\n   510→\n   511→            # Align to go cue\n   512→            aligned = trialtm[spike_mask] - gc\n   513→\n   514→            # Bin spikes\n   515→            counts, _ = np.histogram(aligned, bins=edges)\n   516→\n   517→            # Convert to firing rate and smooth\n   518→            fr = counts.astype(np.float32) / dt\n   519→            fr = smooth_causal(fr, KERNEL, 'reflect')\n   520→            trialdat[ni, :, t] = fr\n   521→\n   522→    return trialdat\n   523→\n   524→\n   525→def compute_mean_fr(trialdat):\n   526→    \"\"\"Compute mean firing rate per neuron across all trials and time bins.\"\"\"\n   527→    # trialdat: (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_trials)\n   528→    return np.mean(np.mean(trialdat, axis=2), axis=1)\n   529→\n   530→\n   531→def find_feature_index(feat_names, target):\n   532→    \"\"\"Find index of target feature in feature name list.\"\"\"\n   533→    target_lower = target.lower()\n   534→    for i, name in enumerate(feat_names):\n   535→        if name.lower().strip() == target_lower:\n   536→            return i\n   537→    return None\n   538→\n   539→\n   540→def compute_velocity_timeseries(session, cam_idx, feat_name, trial_indices, vidshift):\n   541→    \"\"\"Compute velocity for a DLC feature, aligned to neural time bins.\n   542→\n   543→    Returns: (n_timebins, n_trials) arrays of velocity and visibility.\n   544→    \"\"\"\n   545→    n_trials = len(trial_indices)\n   546→    velocity = np.full((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   547→    visible = np.zeros((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), dtype=bool)\n   548→\n   549→    if 'traj' not in session or cam_idx >= len(session['traj']):\n   550→        return velocity, visible\n   551→\n   552→    cam = session['traj'][cam_idx]\n   553→    feat_idx = find_feature_index(cam['featNames'], feat_name)\n   554→    if feat_idx is None:\n   555→        return velocity, visible\n   556→\n   557→    goCue = session['goCue']\n   558→    taxis = TIME_CENTERS\n   559→\n   560→    for out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n   561→        ts = cam['ts'][t]\n   562→        ft = cam['frameTimes'][t]\n   563→        gc = goCue[t]\n   564→\n   565→        if ts is None or ft is None or np.isnan(gc):\n   566→            continue\n   567→        if ft is None or len(ft) == 0:\n   568→            continue\n   569→        if np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n   570→            continue\n   571→\n   572→        # ts shape: (nFeatures, 3, nFrames)\n   573→        x = ts[feat_idx, 0, :]  # x positions\n   574→        y = ts[feat_idx, 1, :]  # y positions\n   575→        conf = ts[feat_idx, 2, :]  # confidence\n   576→\n   577→        # Compute velocity at video frame rate\n   578→        dx = np.diff(x)\n   579→        dy = np.diff(y)\n   580→        v = np.sqrt(dx**2 + dy**2) * VIDEO_FPS  # pixels/second\n   581→        v = np.concatenate([[0], v])  # pad to same length\n   582→\n   583→        # Set velocity to NaN where confidence is low\n   584→        low_conf = conf < DLC_CONF_THRESH\n   585→        v[low_conf] = np.nan\n   586→\n   587→        # Aligned video times\n   588→        aligned_ft = ft - vidshift - gc\n   589→\n   590→        # Interpolate velocity to neural time bins\n   591→        valid = ~np.isnan(v) & ~np.isnan(aligned_ft)\n   592→        if np.sum(valid) < 2:\n   593→            continue\n   594→\n   595→        try:\n   596→            v_interp = np.interp(taxis, aligned_ft[valid], v[valid], left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n   597→            conf_interp = np.interp(taxis, aligned_ft, conf, left=0, right=0)\n   598→        except:\n   599→            continue\n   600→\n   601→        velocity[:, out_idx] = v_interp\n   602→        visible[:, out_idx] = conf_interp >= DLC_CONF_THRESH\n   603→\n   604→    return velocity, visible\n   605→\n   606→\n   607→def compute_motion_energy_timeseries(session, trial_indices, me_trials, vidshift):\n   608→    \"\"\"Align motion energy to neural time bins.\n   609→\n   610→    Returns: (n_timebins, n_trials) array.\n   611→    \"\"\"\n   612→    n_trials = len(trial_indices)\n   613→    me_aligned = np.full((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   614→\n   615→    goCue = session['goCue']\n   616→    taxis = TIME_CENTERS\n   617→\n   618→    if 'traj' not in session or len(session['traj']) == 0:\n   619→        return me_aligned\n   620→\n   621→    cam = session['traj'][0]  # Use camera 0 (bottom) frame times\n   622→\n   623→    for out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n   624→        gc = goCue[t]\n   625→        if np.isnan(gc):\n   626→            continue\n   627→\n   628→        if t >= len(me_trials) or me_trials[t] is None:\n   629→            continue\n   630→\n   631→        me_trial = me_trials[t]\n   632→\n   633→        # Get frame times for alignment\n   634→        ft = cam['frameTimes'][t]\n   635→        if ft is None or len(ft) == 0 or np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n   636→            # Fallback: create frame times assuming 400 Hz\n   637→            n_frames = len(me_trial)\n   638→            ft = np.arange(n_frames) / VIDEO_FPS + 0.5  # 0.5s pad offset\n   639→\n   640→        aligned_ft = ft - vidshift - gc\n   641→\n   642→        # Trim to match me_trial length\n   643→        min_len = min(len(aligned_ft), len(me_trial))\n   644→        aligned_ft = aligned_ft[:min_len]\n   645→        me_data = me_trial[:min_len]\n   646→\n   647→        valid = ~np.isnan(aligned_ft) & ~np.isnan(me_data)\n   648→        if np.sum(valid) < 2:\n   649→            continue\n   650→\n   651→        try:\n   652→            me_interp = np.interp(taxis, aligned_ft[valid], me_data[valid], left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n   653→        except:\n   654→            continue\n   655→\n   656→        me_aligned[:, out_idx] = me_interp\n   657→\n   658→    return me_aligned\n   659→\n   660→\n   661→def discretize_velocity(velocity, visible):\n   662→    \"\"\"Discretize velocity per session.\n   663→\n   664→    0: < 50th percentile (visible)\n   665→    1: >= 50th percentile (visible)\n   666→    2: not visible\n   667→    \"\"\"\n   668→    n_time, n_trials = velocity.shape\n   669→    result = np.full((n_time, n_trials), 2, dtype=np.int32)  # default: not visible\n   670→\n   671→    # Compute 50th percentile from all visible values\n   672→    vis_vals = velocity[visible]\n   673→    if len(vis_vals) == 0:\n   674→        return result\n   675→\n   676→    thresh = np.nanpercentile(vis_vals, 50)\n   677→\n   678→    result[visible & (velocity < thresh)] = 0\n   679→    result[visible & (velocity >= thresh)] = 1\n   680→\n   681→    return result\n   682→\n   683→\n   684→def discretize_motion_energy(me_aligned):\n   685→    \"\"\"Discretize motion energy per session.\n   686→\n   687→    0: < 50th percentile\n   688→    1: >= 50th percentile\n   689→    2: no video (NaN)\n   690→    \"\"\"\n   691→    n_time, n_trials = me_aligned.shape\n   692→    result = np.full((n_time, n_trials), 2, dtype=np.int32)  # default: no video\n   693→\n   694→    valid = ~np.isnan(me_aligned)\n   695→    valid_vals = me_aligned[valid]\n   696→    if len(valid_vals) == 0:\n   697→        return result\n   698→\n   699→    thresh = np.nanpercentile(valid_vals, 50)\n   700→\n   701→    result[valid & (me_aligned < thresh)] = 0\n   702→    result[valid & (me_aligned >= thresh)] = 1\n   703→\n   704→    return result\n   705→\n   706→\n   707→# ============================================================\n   708→# Main conversion\n   709→# ============================================================\n   710→def process_session(fpath, me_path=None, show_processing=False, session_id=''):\n   711→    \"\"\"Process one session and return trial-level data.\"\"\"\n   712→    t0 = time.time()\n   713→\n   714→    # Load\n   715→    session = load_session(fpath)\n   716→    if session is None:\n   717→        return None\n   718→\n   719→    t_load = time.time() - t0\n   720→\n   721→    neurons = session['neurons']\n   722→    if len(neurons) == 0:\n   723→        print(f\"  {session_id}: No neural data, skipping\")\n   724→        return None\n   725→\n   726→    n_trials = session['Ntrials']\n   727→    goCue = session['goCue']\n   728→\n   729→    # ----- Trial filtering -----\n   730→    # Exclude: early lick, stim trials\n   731→    valid_trials = np.ones(n_trials, dtype=bool)\n   732→    valid_trials[session['early']] = False\n   733→    valid_trials[session['stim_enable']] = False\n   734→    # Also exclude trials with NaN goCue\n   735→    valid_trials[np.isnan(goCue)] = False\n   736→\n   737→    trial_indices = np.where(valid_trials)[0]\n   738→    n_valid = len(trial_indices)\n   739→\n   740→    if n_valid < 2:\n   741→        print(f\"  {session_id}: Too few valid trials ({n_valid}), skipping\")\n   742→        return None\n   743→\n   744→    # ----- Neural: align, bin, smooth -----\n   745→    t1 = time.time()\n   746→    trialdat = align_and_bin_spikes(neurons, goCue, n_trials, EDGES, DT)\n   747→    t_bin = time.time() - t1\n   748→\n   749→    # Filter to valid trials\n   750→    trialdat = trialdat[:, :, trial_indices]  # (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_valid)\n   751→\n   752→    # Remove low FR neurons\n   753→    mean_fr = np.mean(np.mean(trialdat, axis=2), axis=1)\n   754→    keep_neurons = mean_fr >= LOW_FR_THRESH\n   755→    trialdat = trialdat[keep_neurons, :, :]\n   756→\n   757→    n_units = trialdat.shape[0]\n   758→    if n_units < MIN_UNITS:\n   759→        print(f\"  {session_id}: Too few units after filtering ({n_units}), skipping\")\n   760→        return None\n   761→\n   762→    print(f\"  {session_id}: {len(neurons)} raw -> {n_units} units, {n_valid} trials \"\n   763→          f\"(load={t_load:.1f}s, bin={t_bin:.1f}s)\")\n   764→\n   765→    # ----- Outputs -----\n   766→    # Lick direction: 0=left, 1=right, 2=none\n   767→    # L/R fields indicate STIMULUS direction, not lick direction\n   768→    # hit: animal licked correct side (L->left, R->right)\n   769→    # miss: animal licked wrong side (L->right, R->left)\n   770→    # no: no lick\n   771→    lick_dir = np.full(n_valid, 2, dtype=np.int32)  # default: none\n   772→    for out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n   773→        if session['hit'][t]:\n   774→            # Correct: lick matches stimulus\n   775→            if session['L'][t]:\n   776→                lick_dir[out_idx] = 0  # left\n   777→            elif session['R'][t]:\n   778→                lick_dir[out_idx] = 1  # right\n   779→        elif session['miss'][t]:\n   780→            # Incorrect: lick is opposite of stimulus\n   781→            if session['L'][t]:\n   782→                lick_dir[out_idx] = 1  # licked right (wrong)\n   783→            elif session['R'][t]:\n   784→                lick_dir[out_idx] = 0  # licked left (wrong)\n   785→        # 'no' trials: lick_dir stays 2 (none)\n   786→\n   787→    # Behavioral context: 0=WC, 1=DR\n   788→    context = np.full(n_valid, 1, dtype=np.int32)  # default: DR\n   789→    for out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n   790→        if session['autowater'][t] == 1:\n   791→            context[out_idx] = 0\n   792→\n   793→    # Outcome: 0=incorrect, 1=correct, 2=ignore\n   794→    outcome = np.full(n_valid, 2, dtype=np.int32)  # default: ignore\n   795→    for out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n   796→        if session['hit'][t]:\n   797→            outcome[out_idx] = 1\n   798→        elif session['miss'][t]:\n   799→            outcome[out_idx] = 0\n   800→\n   801→    # Tongue velocity (bottom camera, 'tongue' feature)\n   802→    vidshift = session.get('vidshift', 0.5)\n   803→    tongue_vel, tongue_vis = compute_velocity_timeseries(\n   804→        session, cam_idx=0, feat_name='tongue',\n   805→        trial_indices=trial_indices, vidshift=vidshift)\n   806→    tongue_disc = discretize_velocity(tongue_vel, tongue_vis)\n   807→\n   808→    # Paw velocity (side camera, 'top_paw' feature)\n   809→    paw_vel, paw_vis = compute_velocity_timeseries(\n   810→        session, cam_idx=1, feat_name='top_paw',\n   811→        trial_indices=trial_indices, vidshift=vidshift)\n   812→    paw_disc = discretize_velocity(paw_vel, paw_vis)\n   813→\n   814→    # Motion energy\n   815→    me_trials = session.get('me_embedded', None)\n   816→    has_me_file = me_path is not None and os.path.exists(me_path)\n   817→\n   818→    if has_me_file:\n   819→        me_trials_loaded, me_thresh = load_motion_energy_file(me_path)\n   820→        me_aligned = compute_motion_energy_timeseries(\n   821→            session, trial_indices, me_trials_loaded, vidshift)\n   822→    elif me_trials is not None:\n   823→        me_aligned = compute_motion_energy_timeseries(\n   824→            session, trial_indices, me_trials, vidshift)\n   825→    else:\n   826→        me_aligned = np.full((N_TIMEBINS, n_valid), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   827→\n   828→    me_disc = discretize_motion_energy(me_aligned)\n   829→\n   830→    # ----- Build per-trial output -----\n   831→    result = {\n   832→        'neural': [],      # list of (n_neurons, n_timebins) arrays\n   833→        'input': [],       # list of (1, n_timebins) arrays (time from go cue)\n   834→        'output': [],      # list of (n_output, n_timebins) or (n_output,) arrays\n   835→        'n_neurons': n_units,\n   836→        'n_trials': n_valid,\n   837→    }\n   838→\n   839→    time_input = TIME_CENTERS.astype(np.float32)  # (n_timebins,)\n   840→\n   841→    for i in range(n_valid):\n   842→        # Neural: (n_neurons, n_timebins)\n   843→        result['neural'].append(trialdat[:, :, i].astype(np.float32))\n   844→\n   845→        # Input: time from go cue (1, n_timebins)\n   846→        result['input'].append(time_input[np.newaxis, :])\n   847→\n   848→        # Output: combine per-trial and time-varying\n   849→        # Per-trial outputs: lick_direction, behavioral_context, outcome\n   850→        # Time-varying outputs: tongue_velocity, paw_velocity, motion_energy\n   851→        out = np.zeros((6, N_TIMEBINS), dtype=np.int32)\n   852→        out[0, :] = lick_dir[i]          # lick direction (per-trial, broadcast)\n   853→        out[1, :] = context[i]           # behavioral context (per-trial, broadcast)\n   854→        out[2, :] = outcome[i]           # outcome (per-trial, broadcast)\n   855→        out[3, :] = tongue_disc[:, i]    # tongue velocity (time-varying)\n   856→        out[4, :] = paw_disc[:, i]       # paw velocity (time-varying)\n   857→        out[5, :] = me_disc[:, i]        # motion energy (time-varying)\n   858→        result['output'].append(out)\n   859→\n   860→    # Visualization\n   861→    if show_processing:\n   862→        plot_processing(session_id, session, trial_indices, trialdat,\n   863→                       tongue_vel, tongue_vis, tongue_disc,\n   864→                       paw_vel, paw_vis, paw_disc,\n   865→                       me_aligned, me_disc, lick_dir, context, outcome)\n   866→\n   867→    return result\n   868→\n   869→\n   870→def (2025)\n\"Separating cognitive and motor processes in the behaving mouse\"\ninto decoder-compatible format.\n\nUsage:\n    python -u convert_data.py <outpicklefile> [--sample] [--full] [--show-processing]\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport pickle\nimport numpy as np\nimport h5py\nimport scipy.io\nimport scipy.signal\nfrom scipy import stats as scipy_stats\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# ============================================================\n# Parameters (matching reference code defaults)\n# ============================================================\nDT = 0.01            # 10 ms time bins (params.dt = 1/100)\nTMIN = -2.5           # seconds before go cue\nTMAX = 2.5            # seconds after go cue\nSMOOTH_WIN = 15       # causal Gaussian window size (samples)\nLOW_FR_THRESH = 1.0   # Hz, minimum mean firing rate\nMIN_UNITS = 10        # minimum units per session\nDLC_CONF_THRESH = 0.5 # DLC confidence threshold for visibility\nVIDEO_FPS = 400       # video frame rate\n\n# Quality labels to EXCLUDE (matching findClusters 'all')\nEXCLUDE_QUALITY = {'garbage', 'noisy', 'gabrga', 'real?', ''}\n\n# Time bin edges and centers\nEDGES = np.arange(TMIN, TMAX + DT, DT)\nTIME_CENTERS = EDGES[:-1] + DT / 2\nN_TIMEBINS = len(TIME_CENTERS)\n\n# Data directories\nDATA_DIRS = {\n    'Ephys': '/app/data/Ephys_Behavior',\n    'RandDelay': '/app/data/RandomizedDelay_Ephys_Behavior',\n}\n\n# Duplicate files to skip (same session data saved twice with different dates/formats)\nSKIP_FILES = {\n    'data_structure_JEB23_2023-10-20.mat',  # duplicate of JEB23_2023-10-19\n}\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# Causal Gaussian smoothing (matching mySmooth.m)\n# ============================================================\ndef causal_gaussian_kernel(N):\n    \"\"\"Create causal Gaussian kernel matching MATLAB gausswin + causal zeroing.\"\"\"\n    if N <= 1:\n        return np.array([1.0])\n    # MATLAB gausswin(N) = exp(-0.5 * ((n - (N-1)/2) / (alpha * (N-1)/2))^2)\n    # where alpha = 2.5 by default\n    alpha = 2.5\n    n = np.arange(N)\n    w = np.exp(-0.5 * ((n - (N - 1) / 2) / (alpha * (N - 1) / 2)) ** 2)\n    # Zero out first half (causal)\n    w[:N // 2] = 0\n    # Normalize\n    w = w / w.sum()\n    return w\n\nKERNEL = causal_gaussian_kernel(SMOOTH_WIN)\n\n\ndef smooth_causal(x, kernel=KERNEL, bctype='reflect'):\n    \"\"\"Apply causal Gaussian smoothing with boundary handling.\n\n    Matches mySmooth.m: prepend reflected/zero data, convolve 'same', trim.\n    x: (n_time,) or (n_time, n_cols) array\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(kernel) <= 1:\n        return x\n\n    was_1d = x.ndim == 1\n    if was_1d:\n        x = x[:, np.newaxis]\n\n    N = len(kernel)\n    if bctype == 'reflect':\n        pad = x[:N, :]\n        x_filt = np.concatenate([pad, x], axis=0)\n        trim = N\n    elif bctype == 'zeropad':\n        pad = np.zeros((N, x.shape[1]))\n        x_filt = np.concatenate([pad, x], axis=0)\n        trim = N\n    else:\n        x_filt = x\n        trim = 0\n\n    out = np.zeros_like(x_filt)\n    for j in range(x_filt.shape[1]):\n        out[:, j] = np.convolve(x_filt[:, j], kernel, mode='same')\n    out = out[trim:, :]\n\n    if was_1d:\n        out = out[:, 0]\n    return out\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# Data loading helpers\n# ============================================================\ndef read_h5_string(f, ref):\n    \"\"\"Read a string stored as uint16 array via h5py reference.\"\"\"\n    data = f[ref][:]\n    return ''.join(chr(c) for c in data.flatten())\n\n\ndef load_session_h5(fpath):\n    \"\"\"Load session data from HDF5 MATLAB v7.3 file.\"\"\"\n    session = {}\n    with h5py.File(fpath, 'r') as f:\n        obj = f['obj']\n\n        # ----- Behavioral data -----\n        bp = obj['bp']\n        session['Ntrials'] = int(np.array(bp['Ntrials']).flatten()[0])\n        session['hit'] = np.array(bp['hit']).flatten().astype(bool)\n        session['miss'] = np.array(bp['miss']).flatten().astype(bool)\n        session['no'] = np.array(bp['no']).flatten().astype(bool)\n        session['early'] = np.array(bp['early']).flatten().astype(bool)\n        session['autowater'] = np.array(bp['autowater']).flatten().astype(int)\n        session['L'] = np.array(bp['L']).flatten().astype(bool)\n        session['R'] = np.array(bp['R']).flatten().astype(bool)\n\n        # Stim\n        session['stim_enable'] = np.array(bp['stim']['enable']).flatten().astype(bool)\n\n        # Event times\n        ev = bp['ev']\n        session['goCue'] = np.array(ev['goCue']).flatten()\n        session['sample'] = np.array(ev['sample']).flatten()\n        session['delay'] = np.array(ev['delay']).flatten()\n\n        # Lick times (cell arrays of variable-length arrays)\n        n_trials = session['Ntrials']\n        session['lickL'] = []\n        session['lickR'] = []\n        lickL_ds = ev['lickL']\n        lickR_ds = ev['lickR']\n        for t in range(n_trials):\n            try:\n                ref = lickL_ds[0, t] if lickL_ds.ndim > 1 else lickL_ds[t]\n                licks = np.array(f[ref]).flatten()\n                session['lickL'].append(licks)\n            except:\n                session['lickL'].append(np.array([]))\n            try:\n                ref = lickR_ds[0, t] if lickR_ds.ndim > 1 else lickR_ds[t]\n                licks = np.array(f[ref]).flatten()\n                session['lickR'].append(licks)\n            except:\n                session['lickR'].append(np.array([]))\n\n        # ----- Neural data -----\n        if 'clu' not in obj:\n            session['neurons'] = []\n            return session\n\n        clu_ds = obj['clu']\n        neurons = []\n        for pi in range(clu_ds.shape[0]):\n            for pj in range(clu_ds.shape[1]):\n                ref = clu_ds[pi, pj]\n                dereffed = f[ref]\n                if not isinstance(dereffed, h5py.Group) or 'quality' not in dereffed:\n                    continue\n\n                quality_ds = dereffed['quality']\n                n_neurons = quality_ds.shape[0]\n\n                for ni in range(n_neurons):\n                    # Read quality string\n                    q_ref = quality_ds[ni, 0]\n                    quality = read_h5_string(f, q_ref).strip().lower()\n\n                    if quality in EXCLUDE_QUALITY:\n                        continue\n\n                    # Read spike data\n                    tm_ref = dereffed['tm'][ni, 0]\n                    trialtm_ref = dereffed['trialtm'][ni, 0]\n                    trial_ref = dereffed['trial'][ni, 0]\n\n                    tm = np.array(f[tm_ref]).flatten()\n                    trialtm = np.array(f[trialtm_ref]).flatten()\n                    trial = np.array(f[trial_ref]).flatten().astype(int)\n\n                    neurons.append({\n                        'quality': quality,\n                        'trialtm': trialtm,\n                        'trial': trial,\n                    })\n\n        session['neurons'] = neurons\n\n        # ----- Video tracking data -----\n        if 'traj' in obj:\n            traj_ds = obj['traj']\n            session['traj'] = []\n            for cam_idx in range(traj_ds.shape[0]):\n                cam_ref = traj_ds[cam_idx, 0]\n                cam = f[cam_ref]\n\n                cam_data = {'ts': [], 'frameTimes': [], 'featNames': []}\n\n                # Feature names (from first trial)\n                feat_refs = cam['featNames']\n                feat_ref = feat_refs[0, 0]\n                feat_data = f[feat_ref]\n                n_features = feat_data.shape[1] if feat_data.ndim > 1 else feat_data.shape[0]\n                names = []\n                for i in range(n_features):\n                    idx = (0, i) if feat_data.ndim > 1 else (i,)\n                    name_ref = feat_data[idx]\n                    name = read_h5_string(f, name_ref)\n                    names.append(name)\n                cam_data['featNames'] = names\n\n                # Per-trial ts and frameTimes\n                ts_ds = cam['ts']\n                ft_ds = cam['frameTimes']\n\n                for t in range(n_trials):\n                    try:\n                        ts_ref = ts_ds[t, 0]\n                        ts = np.array(f[ts_ref])  # (nFeatures, 3, nFrames)\n                        cam_data['ts'].append(ts)\n                    except:\n                        cam_data['ts'].append(None)\n\n                    try:\n                        ft_ref = ft_ds[t, 0]\n                        ft = np.array(f[ft_ref]).flatten()\n                        cam_data['frameTimes'].append(ft)\n                    except:\n                        cam_data['frameTimes'].append(None)\n\n                session['traj'].append(cam_data)\n\n        # ----- Video offset computation -----\n        try:\n            sglx = obj['sglx']\n            bp_bitstart = np.array(ev['bitStart']).flatten()\n            sglx_bitstart = np.array(sglx['bitcode']['bitstart']).flatten()\n            sglx_fs = np.array(sglx['fs']).flatten()[0]\n\n            mode_bp = scipy_stats.mode(bp_bitstart[~np.isnan(bp_bitstart)], keepdims=False).mode\n            mode_sglx = scipy_stats.mode(sglx_bitstart[~np.isnan(sglx_bitstart)], keepdims=False).mode\n            session['vidshift'] = mode_sglx / sglx_fs - mode_bp\n        except:\n            session['vidshift'] = 0.5  # default padSec\n\n        # ----- Motion energy (if embedded in obj.me) -----\n        if 'me' in obj:\n            me_ds = obj['me']\n            me_data = []\n            if isinstance(me_ds, h5py.Dataset):\n                # Array of refs\n                for t in range(n_trials):\n                    try:\n                        ref = me_ds[0, t] if me_ds.ndim > 1 else me_ds[t]\n                        me_trial = np.array(f[ref]).flatten()\n                        me_data.append(me_trial)\n                    except:\n                        me_data.append(None)\n            session['me_embedded'] = me_data\n\n    return session\n\n\ndef _has_field(obj, name):\n    \"\"\"Check if numpy.void or structured array has a named field.\"\"\"\n    if isinstance(obj, np.void) and obj.dtype.names is not None:\n        return name in obj.dtype.names\n    if isinstance(obj, dict):\n        return name in obj\n    return False\n\n\ndef load_session_scipy(fpath):\n    \"\"\"Load session data from older MATLAB format file.\"\"\"\n    d = scipy.io.loadmat(fpath, simplify_cells=False)\n    obj = d['obj'][0, 0]  # numpy.void\n    bp = obj['bp'][0, 0]  # numpy.void\n\n    session = {}\n    n_trials = int(np.array(bp['Ntrials']).flatten()[0])\n    session['Ntrials'] = n_trials\n    session['hit'] = np.array(bp['hit']).flatten().astype(bool)\n    session['miss'] = np.array(bp['miss']).flatten().astype(bool)\n    session['no'] = np.array(bp['no']).flatten().astype(bool)\n    session['early'] = np.array(bp['early']).flatten().astype(bool)\n    session['autowater'] = np.array(bp['autowater']).flatten().astype(int)\n    session['L'] = np.array(bp['L']).flatten().astype(bool)\n    session['R'] = np.array(bp['R']).flatten().astype(bool)\n\n    stim = bp['stim'][0, 0]\n    session['stim_enable'] = np.array(stim['enable']).flatten().astype(bool)\n\n    ev = bp['ev'][0, 0]\n    session['goCue'] = np.array(ev['goCue']).flatten()\n    session['sample'] = np.array(ev['sample']).flatten()\n    session['delay'] = np.array(ev['delay']).flatten()\n\n    # Lick times\n    for side in ['lickL', 'lickR']:\n        lick_list = []\n        if _has_field(ev, side):\n            lick_data = ev[side].flatten()\n            for t in range(n_trials):\n                try:\n                    licks = np.array(lick_data[t]).flatten() if t < len(lick_data) else np.array([])\n                    licks = licks[~np.isnan(licks)] if len(licks) > 0 else np.array([])\n                    lick_list.append(licks)\n                except:\n                    lick_list.append(np.array([]))\n        else:\n            lick_list = [np.array([])] * n_trials\n        session[side] = lick_list\n\n    # Neural data - clu is (1, nProbes) object array, each element is structured array (nNeurons,)\n    neurons = []\n    if _has_field(obj, 'clu'):\n        clu_raw = obj['clu']\n        for probe_data in clu_raw.flatten():\n            if probe_data is None or (isinstance(probe_data, np.ndarray) and probe_data.size == 0):\n                continue\n            # probe_data: structured array of neurons with fields (tm, quality, trialtm, trial, ...)\n            if hasattr(probe_data, 'dtype') and probe_data.dtype.names is not None:\n                neuron_arr = probe_data.flatten()\n                for ni in range(len(neuron_arr)):\n                    neuron = neuron_arr[ni]\n                    q_raw = neuron['quality']\n                    quality = str(np.array(q_raw).flat[0]).strip().lower() if np.array(q_raw).size > 0 else ''\n                    if quality in EXCLUDE_QUALITY:\n                        continue\n                    trialtm = np.array(neuron['trialtm']).flatten()\n                    trial = np.array(neuron['trial']).flatten().astype(int)\n                    neurons.append({'quality': quality, 'trialtm': trialtm, 'trial': trial})\n\n    session['neurons'] = neurons\n\n    # Video tracking - traj is (1, nCameras) object array\n    if _has_field(obj, 'traj'):\n        session['traj'] = []\n        traj_raw = obj['traj']\n        for cam_idx in range(traj_raw.size):\n            cam_arr = traj_raw.flat[cam_idx]\n            cam_out = {'ts': [], 'frameTimes': [], 'featNames': []}\n\n            if cam_arr is None or (isinstance(cam_arr, np.ndarray) and cam_arr.size == 0):\n                session['traj'].append(cam_out)\n                continue\n\n            # cam_arr: structured array (1, nTrials) with fields per trial\n            if hasattr(cam_arr, 'dtype') and cam_arr.dtype.names is not None:\n                cam_flat = cam_arr.flatten()\n\n                # Feature names from first trial\n                for t_check in range(min(len(cam_flat), n_trials)):\n                    fn_field = cam_flat[t_check]['featNames']\n                    if fn_field is not None and np.array(fn_field).size > 0:\n                        fn_flat = np.array(fn_field).flatten()\n                        names = [str(np.array(fn_flat[i]).flat[0]).strip()\n                                if np.array(fn_flat[i]).size > 0 else ''\n                                for i in range(len(fn_flat))]\n                        cam_out['featNames'] = names\n                        break\n\n                # Per-trial ts and frameTimes\n                for t in range(min(len(cam_flat), n_trials)):\n                    trial_struct = cam_flat[t]\n\n                    # ts: (nFrames, 3, nFeatures) -> transpose to (nFeatures, 3, nFrames)\n                    ts_val = trial_struct['ts']\n                    if isinstance(ts_val, np.ndarray) and ts_val.ndim == 3 and ts_val.size > 0:\n                        cam_out['ts'].append(ts_val.transpose(2, 1, 0))\n                    else:\n                        cam_out['ts'].append(None)\n\n                    ft_val = trial_struct['frameTimes']\n                    if isinstance(ft_val, np.ndarray) and ft_val.size > 0:\n                        cam_out['frameTimes'].append(ft_val.flatten())\n                    else:\n                        cam_out['frameTimes'].append(None)\n\n                # Pad if fewer trials in traj\n                while len(cam_out['ts']) < n_trials:\n                    cam_out['ts'].append(None)\n                    cam_out['frameTimes'].append(None)\n\n            session['traj'].append(cam_out)\n\n    # Video offset\n    try:\n        sglx = obj['sglx'][0, 0]\n        bp_bitstart = np.array(ev['bitStart']).flatten()\n        bc = sglx['bitcode'][0, 0]\n        sglx_bitstart = np.array(bc['bitstart']).flatten()\n        sglx_fs = float(np.array(sglx['fs']).flatten()[0])\n        mode_bp = scipy_stats.mode(bp_bitstart[~np.isnan(bp_bitstart)], keepdims=False).mode\n        mode_sglx = scipy_stats.mode(sglx_bitstart[~np.isnan(sglx_bitstart)], keepdims=False).mode\n        session['vidshift'] = mode_sglx / sglx_fs - mode_bp\n    except:\n        session['vidshift'] = 0.5\n\n    # Motion energy (embedded in obj.me)\n    if _has_field(obj, 'me'):\n        me_raw = obj['me']\n        me_data = []\n        me_flat = me_raw.flatten()\n        for t in range(n_trials):\n            try:\n                me_trial = np.array(me_flat[t]).flatten() if t < len(me_flat) else None\n                me_data.append(me_trial)\n            except:\n                me_data.append(None)\n        session['me_embedded'] = me_data\n\n    return session\n\n\ndef load_motion_energy_file(me_path):\n    \"\"\"Load motion energy from separate .mat file.\"\"\"\n    d = scipy.io.loadmat(me_path, simplify_cells=True)\n    me = d['me']\n\n    # Format 1: struct with 'data' and 'moveThresh' fields\n    if isinstance(me, dict) and 'data' in me:\n        data = me['data']\n        thresh = me.get('moveThresh', 10)\n    elif isinstance(me, np.ndarray) and me.dtype == object:\n        # Format 2: just an array of per-trial arrays (no struct wrapper)\n        data = me\n        thresh = 10  # default\n    else:\n        return [], 10.0\n\n    me_trials = []\n    if isinstance(data, list):\n        for trial_data in data:\n            me_trials.append(np.array(trial_data).flatten() if trial_data is not None else None)\n    elif isinstance(data, np.ndarray):\n        if data.dtype == object:\n            for trial_data in data.flatten():\n                me_trials.append(np.array(trial_data).flatten() if trial_data is not None else None)\n        else:\n            me_trials.append(data.flatten())\n\n    return me_trials, float(thresh)\n\n\ndef load_session(fpath):\n    \"\"\"Load session from either h5py or scipy format.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        session = load_session_h5(fpath)\n        return session\n    except:\n        pass\n    try:\n        session = load_session_scipy(fpath)\n        return session\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  ERROR loading {fpath}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# Processing functions\n# ============================================================\ndef align_and_bin_spikes(neurons, goCue, n_trials, edges, dt):\n    \"\"\"Align spikes to go cue and bin into time bins.\n\n    Returns: (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_trials) array of firing rates in Hz.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_timebins = len(edges) - 1\n    n_neurons_total = len(neurons)\n\n    # Pre-allocate\n    trialdat = np.zeros((n_neurons_total, n_timebins, n_trials), dtype=np.float32)\n\n    for ni, neuron in enumerate(neurons):\n        trialtm = neuron['trialtm']\n        trial = neuron['trial']\n\n        for t in range(n_trials):\n            trial_num = t + 1  # MATLAB 1-indexed\n            gc = goCue[t]\n            if np.isnan(gc):\n                continue\n\n            # Get spikes for this trial\n            spike_mask = trial == trial_num\n            if not np.any(spike_mask):\n                continue\n\n            # Align to go cue\n            aligned = trialtm[spike_mask] - gc\n\n            # Bin spikes\n            counts, _ = np.histogram(aligned, bins=edges)\n\n            # Convert to firing rate and smooth\n            fr = counts.astype(np.float32) / dt\n            fr = smooth_causal(fr, KERNEL, 'reflect')\n            trialdat[ni, :, t] = fr\n\n    return trialdat\n\n\ndef compute_mean_fr(trialdat):\n    \"\"\"Compute mean firing rate per neuron across all trials and time bins.\"\"\"\n    # trialdat: (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_trials)\n    return np.mean(np.mean(trialdat, axis=2), axis=1)\n\n\ndef find_feature_index(feat_names, target):\n    \"\"\"Find index of target feature in feature name list.\"\"\"\n    target_lower = target.lower()\n    for i, name in enumerate(feat_names):\n        if name.lower().strip() == target_lower:\n            return i\n    return None\n\n\ndef compute_velocity_timeseries(session, cam_idx, feat_name, trial_indices, vidshift):\n    \"\"\"Compute velocity for a DLC feature, aligned to neural time bins.\n\n    Returns: (n_timebins, n_trials) arrays of velocity and visibility.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(trial_indices)\n    velocity = np.full((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n    visible = np.zeros((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), dtype=bool)\n\n    if 'traj' not in session or cam_idx >= len(session['traj']):\n        return velocity, visible\n\n    cam = session['traj'][cam_idx]\n    feat_idx = find_feature_index(cam['featNames'], feat_name)\n    if feat_idx is None:\n        return velocity, visible\n\n    goCue = session['goCue']\n    taxis = TIME_CENTERS\n\n    for out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n        ts = cam['ts'][t]\n        ft = cam['frameTimes'][t]\n        gc = goCue[t]\n\n        if ts is None or ft is None or np.isnan(gc):\n            continue\n        if ft is None or len(ft) == 0:\n            continue\n        if np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n            continue\n\n        # ts shape: (nFeatures, 3, nFrames)\n        x = ts[feat_idx, 0, :]  # x positions\n        y = ts[feat_idx, 1, :]  # y positions\n        conf = ts[feat_idx, 2, :]  # confidence\n\n        # Compute velocity at video frame rate\n        dx = np.diff(x)\n        dy = np.diff(y)\n        v = np.sqrt(dx**2 + dy**2) * VIDEO_FPS  # pixels/second\n        v = np.concatenate([[0], v])  # pad to same length\n\n        # Set velocity to NaN where confidence is low\n        low_conf = conf < DLC_CONF_THRESH\n        v[low_conf] = np.nan\n\n        # Aligned video times\n        aligned_ft = ft - vidshift - gc\n\n        # Interpolate velocity to neural time bins\n        valid = ~np.isnan(v) & ~np.isnan(aligned_ft)\n        if np.sum(valid) < 2:\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            v_interp = np.interp(taxis, aligned_ft[valid], v[valid], left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n            conf_interp = np.interp(taxis, aligned_ft, conf, left=0, right=0)\n        except:\n            continue\n\n        velocity[:, out_idx] = v_interp\n        visible[:, out_idx] = conf_interp >= DLC_CONF_THRESH\n\n    return velocity, visible\n\n\ndef compute_motion_energy_timeseries(session, trial_indices, me_trials, vidshift):\n    \"\"\"Align motion energy to neural time bins.\n\n    Returns: (n_timebins, n_trials) array.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(trial_indices)\n    me_aligned = np.full((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    goCue = session['goCue']\n    taxis = TIME_CENTERS\n\n    if 'traj' not in session or len(session['traj']) == 0:\n        return me_aligned\n\n    cam = session['traj'][0]  # Use camera 0 (bottom) frame times\n\n    for out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n        gc = goCue[t]\n        if np.isnan(gc):\n            continue\n\n        if t >= len(me_trials) or me_trials[t] is None:\n            continue\n\n        me_trial = me_trials[t]\n\n        # Get frame times for alignment\n        ft = cam['frameTimes'][t]\n        if ft is None or len(ft) == 0 or np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n            # Fallback: create frame times assuming 400 Hz\n            n_frames = len(me_trial)\n            ft = np.arange(n_frames) / VIDEO_FPS + 0.5  # 0.5s pad offset\n\n        aligned_ft = ft - vidshift - gc\n\n        # Trim to match me_trial length\n        min_len = min(len(aligned_ft), len(me_trial))\n        aligned_ft = aligned_ft[:min_len]\n        me_data = me_trial[:min_len]\n\n        valid = ~np.isnan(aligned_ft) & ~np.isnan(me_data)\n        if np.sum(valid) < 2:\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            me_interp = np.interp(taxis, aligned_ft[valid], me_data[valid], left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n        except:\n            continue\n\n        me_aligned[:, out_idx] = me_interp\n\n    return me_aligned\n\n\ndef discretize_velocity(velocity, visible):\n    \"\"\"Discretize velocity per session.\n\n    0: < 50th percentile (visible)\n    1: >= 50th percentile (visible)\n    2: not visible\n    \"\"\"\n    n_time, n_trials = velocity.shape\n    result = np.full((n_time, n_trials), 2, dtype=np.int32)  # default: not visible\n\n    # Compute 50th percentile from all visible values\n    vis_vals = velocity[visible]\n    if len(vis_vals) == 0:\n        return result\n\n    thresh = np.nanpercentile(vis_vals, 50)\n\n    result[visible & (velocity < thresh)] = 0\n    result[visible & (velocity >= thresh)] = 1\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef discretize_motion_energy(me_aligned):\n    \"\"\"Discretize motion energy per session.\n\n    0: < 50th percentile\n    1: >= 50th percentile\n    2: no video (NaN)\n    \"\"\"\n    n_time, n_trials = me_aligned.shape\n    result = np.full((n_time, n_trials), 2, dtype=np.int32)  # default: no video\n\n    valid = ~np.isnan(me_aligned)\n    valid_vals = me_aligned[valid]\n    if len(valid_vals) == 0:\n        return result\n\n    thresh = np.nanpercentile(valid_vals, 50)\n\n    result[valid & (me_aligned < thresh)] = 0\n    result[valid & (me_aligned >= thresh)] = 1\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# Main conversion\n# ============================================================\ndef process_session(fpath, me_path=None, show_processing=False, session_id=''):\n    \"\"\"Process one session and return trial-level data.\"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    # Load\n    session = load_session(fpath)\n    if session is None:\n        return None\n\n    t_load = time.time() - t0\n\n    neurons = session['neurons']\n    if len(neurons) == 0:\n        print(f\"  {session_id}: No neural data, skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    n_trials = session['Ntrials']\n    goCue = session['goCue']\n\n    # ----- Trial filtering -----\n    # Exclude: early lick, stim trials\n    valid_trials = np.ones(n_trials, dtype=bool)\n    valid_trials[session['early']] = False\n    valid_trials[session['stim_enable']] = False\n    # Also exclude trials with NaN goCue\n    valid_trials[np.isnan(goCue)] = False\n\n    trial_indices = np.where(valid_trials)[0]\n    n_valid = len(trial_indices)\n\n    if n_valid < 2:\n        print(f\"  {session_id}: Too few valid trials ({n_valid}), skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    # ----- Neural: align, bin, smooth -----\n    t1 = time.time()\n    trialdat = align_and_bin_spikes(neurons, goCue, n_trials, EDGES, DT)\n    t_bin = time.time() - t1\n\n    # Filter to valid trials\n    trialdat = trialdat[:, :, trial_indices]  # (n_neurons, n_timebins, n_valid)\n\n    # Remove low FR neurons\n    mean_fr = np.mean(np.mean(trialdat, axis=2), axis=1)\n    keep_neurons = mean_fr >= LOW_FR_THRESH\n    trialdat = trialdat[keep_neurons, :, :]\n\n    n_units = trialdat.shape[0]\n    if n_units < MIN_UNITS:\n        print(f\"  {session_id}: Too few units after filtering ({n_units}), skipping\")\n        return None\n\n    print(f\"  {session_id}: {len(neurons)} raw -> {n_units} units, {n_valid} trials \"\n          f\"(load={t_load:.1f}s, bin={t_bin:.1f}s)\")\n\n    # ----- Outputs -----\n    # Lick direction: 0=left, 1=right, 2=none\n    # L/R fields indicate STIMULUS direction, not lick direction\n    # hit: animal licked correct side (L->left, R->right)\n    # miss: animal licked wrong side (L->right, R->left)\n    # no: no lick\n    lick_dir = np.full(n_valid, 2, dtype=np.int32)  # default: none\n    for out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n        if session['hit'][t]:\n            # Correct: lick matches stimulus\n            if session['L'][t]:\n                lick_dir[out_idx] = 0  # left\n            elif session['R'][t]:\n                lick_dir[out_idx] = 1  # right\n        elif session['miss'][t]:\n            # Incorrect: lick is opposite of stimulus\n            if session['L'][t]:\n                lick_dir[out_idx] = 1  # licked right (wrong)\n            elif session['R'][t]:\n                lick_dir[out_idx] = 0  # licked left (wrong)\n        # 'no' trials: lick_dir stays 2 (none)\n\n    # Behavioral context: 0=WC, 1=DR\n    context = np.full(n_valid, 1, dtype=np.int32)  # default: DR\n    for out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n        if session['autowater'][t] == 1:\n            context[out_idx] = 0\n\n    # Outcome: 0=incorrect, 1=correct, 2=ignore\n    outcome = np.full(n_valid, 2, dtype=np.int32)  # default: ignore\n    for out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n        if session['hit'][t]:\n            outcome[out_idx] = 1\n        elif session['miss'][t]:\n            outcome[out_idx] = 0\n\n    # Tongue velocity (bottom camera, 'tongue' feature)\n    vidshift = session.get('vidshift', 0.5)\n    tongue_vel, tongue_vis = compute_velocity_timeseries(\n        session, cam_idx=0, feat_name='tongue',\n        trial_indices=trial_indices, vidshift=vidshift)\n    tongue_disc = discretize_velocity(tongue_vel, tongue_vis)\n\n    # Paw velocity (side camera, 'top_paw' feature)\n    paw_vel, paw_vis = compute_velocity_timeseries(\n        session, cam_idx=1, feat_name='top_paw',\n        trial_indices=trial_indices, vidshift=vidshift)\n    paw_disc = discretize_velocity(paw_vel, paw_vis)\n\n    # Motion energy\n    me_trials = session.get('me_embedded', None)\n    has_me_file = me_path is not None and os.path.exists(me_path)\n\n    if has_me_file:\n        me_trials_loaded, me_thresh = load_motion_energy_file(me_path)\n        me_aligned = compute_motion_energy_timeseries(\n            session, trial_indices, me_trials_loaded, vidshift)\n    elif me_trials is not None:\n        me_aligned = compute_motion_energy_timeseries(\n            session, trial_indices, me_trials, vidshift)\n    else:\n        me_aligned = np.full((N_TIMEBINS, n_valid), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    me_disc = discretize_motion_energy(me_aligned)\n\n    # ----- Build per-trial output -----\n    result = {\n        'neural': [],      # list of (n_neurons, n_timebins) arrays\n        'input': [],       # list of (1, n_timebins) arrays (time from go cue)\n        'output': [],      # list of (n_output, n_timebins) or (n_output,) arrays\n        'n_neurons': n_units,\n        'n_trials': n_valid,\n    }\n\n    time_input = TIME_CENTERS.astype(np.float32)  # (n_timebins,)\n\n    for i in range(n_valid):\n        # Neural: (n_neurons, n_timebins)\n        result['neural'].append(trialdat[:, :, i].astype(np.float32))\n\n        # Input: time from go cue (1, n_timebins)\n        result['input'].append(time_input[np.newaxis, :])\n\n        # Output: combine per-trial and time-varying\n        # Per-trial outputs: lick_direction, behavioral_context, outcome\n        # Time-varying outputs: tongue_velocity, paw_velocity, motion_energy\n        out = np.zeros((6, N_TIMEBINS), dtype=np.int32)\n        out[0, :] = lick_dir[i]          # lick direction (per-trial, broadcast)\n        out[1, :] = context[i]           # behavioral context (per-trial, broadcast)\n        out[2, :] = outcome[i]           # outcome (per-trial, broadcast)\n        out[3, :] = tongue_disc[:, i]    # tongue velocity (time-varying)\n        out[4, :] = paw_disc[:, i]       # paw velocity (time-varying)\n        out[5, :] = me_disc[:, i]        # motion energy (time-varying)\n        result['output'].append(out)\n\n    # Visualization\n    if show_processing:\n        plot_processing(session_id, session, trial_indices, trialdat,\n                       tongue_vel, tongue_vis, tongue_disc,\n                       paw_vel, paw_vis, paw_disc,\n                       me_aligned, me_disc, lick_dir, context, outcome)\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef plot_processing(session_id, session, trial_indices, trialdat,\n                   tongue_vel, tongue_vis, tongue_disc,\n                   paw_vel, paw_vis, paw_disc,\n                   me_aligned, me_disc, lick_dir, context, outcome):\n    \"\"\"Plot processing visualizations.\"\"\"\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(6, 2, figsize=(20, 24))\n    fig.suptitle(f'Processing: {session_id}', fontsize=16)\n\n    taxis = TIME_CENTERS\n    n_trials = trialdat.shape[2]\n\n    # 1. Neural raster (first 20 neurons, first trial)\n    ax = axes[0, 0]\n    trial_idx = min(5, n_trials - 1)\n    n_show = min(20, trialdat.shape[0])\n    im = ax.imshow(trialdat[:n_show, :, trial_idx], aspect='auto',\n                   extent=[TMIN, TMAX, n_show, 0], cmap='hot')\n    ax.set_title(f'Neural activity (trial {trial_idx}, first {n_show} neurons)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Neuron')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='white', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)\n    plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax, label='FR (Hz)')\n\n    # 2. Mean neural activity across trials\n    ax = axes[0, 1]\n    mean_fr = np.mean(trialdat, axis=2)  # (neurons, time)\n    pop_mean = np.mean(mean_fr, axis=0)\n    ax.plot(taxis, pop_mean)\n    ax.set_title('Population mean firing rate')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Mean FR (Hz)')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)\n\n    # 3. Tongue velocity\n    ax = axes[1, 0]\n    if not np.all(np.isnan(tongue_vel)):\n        ax.plot(taxis, np.nanmean(tongue_vel, axis=1), label='Mean velocity')\n        ax.set_title('Tongue velocity (mean across trials)')\n    else:\n        ax.set_title('Tongue velocity (no data)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)\n\n    # 4. Tongue discretized\n    ax = axes[1, 1]\n    for val, label in [(0, '<50th'), (1, '>=50th'), (2, 'not visible')]:\n        frac = np.mean(tongue_disc == val, axis=1)\n        ax.plot(taxis, frac, label=label)\n    ax.set_title('Tongue velocity discretized')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.legend()\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)\n\n    # 5. Paw velocity\n    ax = axes[2, 0]\n    if not np.all(np.isnan(paw_vel)):\n        ax.plot(taxis, np.nanmean(paw_vel, axis=1))\n        ax.set_title('Paw velocity (mean across trials)')\n    else:\n        ax.set_title('Paw velocity (no data)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)\n\n    # 6. Paw discretized\n    ax = axes[2, 1]\n    for val, label in [(0, '<50th'), (1, '>=50th'), (2, 'not visible')]:\n        frac = np.mean(paw_disc == val, axis=1)\n        ax.plot(taxis, frac, label=label)\n    ax.set_title('Paw velocity discretized')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.legend()\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)\n\n    # 7. Motion energy\n    ax = axes[3, 0]\n    if not np.all(np.isnan(me_aligned)):\n        ax.plot(taxis, np.nanmean(me_aligned, axis=1))\n        ax.set_title('Motion energy (mean across trials)')\n    else:\n        ax.set_title('Motion energy (no data)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)\n\n    # 8. Motion energy discretized\n    ax = axes[3, 1]\n    for val, label in [(0, '<50th'), (1, '>=50th'), (2, 'no video')]:\n        frac = np.mean(me_disc == val, axis=1)\n        ax.plot(taxis, frac, label=label)\n    ax.set_title('Motion energy discretized')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Time from go cue (s)')\n    ax.legend()\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7)\n\n    # 9. Output distributions\n    ax = axes[4, 0]\n    labels = ['left', 'right', 'none']\n    counts = [np.sum(lick_dir == i) for i in range(3)]\n    ax.bar(labels, counts)\n    ax.set_title('Lick direction distribution')\n\n    ax = axes[4, 1]\n    labels = ['WC', 'DR']\n    counts = [np.sum(context == i) for i in range(2)]\n    ax.bar(labels, counts)\n    ax.set_title('Behavioral context distribution')\n\n    # 10. Outcome distribution\n    ax = axes[5, 0]\n    labels = ['incorrect', 'correct', 'ignore']\n    counts = [np.sum(outcome == i) for i in range(3)]\n    ax.bar(labels, counts)\n    ax.set_title('Outcome distribution')\n\n    axes[5, 1].axis('off')\n\n    fig.tight_layout()\n    safe_id = session_id.replace('/', '_').replace(' ', '_')\n    fig.savefig(f'/app/processing_{safe_id}.png', dpi=100)\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print(f\"  Saved processing plot: /app/processing_{safe_id}.png\")\n\n\ndef find_me_file(data_dir, animal, date):\n    \"\"\"Find matching motionEnergy file.\"\"\"\n    pattern = f'motionEnergy_{animal}_{date}.mat'\n    fpath = os.path.join(data_dir, pattern)\n    if os.path.exists(fpath):\n        return fpath\n    return None\n\n\ndef discover_sessions(sample=False):\n    \"\"\"Discover all session files.\"\"\"\n    sessions = []\n\n    for dir_key, data_dir in DATA_DIRS.items():\n        if not os.path.exists(data_dir):\n            continue\n\n        for fn in sorted(os.listdir(data_dir)):\n            if not fn.startswith('data_structure_'):\n                continue\n            if fn in SKIP_FILES:\n                continue\n\n            parts = fn.replace('.mat', '').split('_')\n            animal = parts[2]\n            date = parts[3]\n\n            fpath = os.path.join(data_dir, fn)\n            me_path = find_me_file(data_dir, animal, date)\n\n            sessions.append({\n                'fpath': fpath,\n                'me_path': me_path,\n                'animal': animal,\n                'date': date,\n                'dir': dir_key,\n                'session_id': f'{animal}_{date}',\n            })\n\n    if sample:\n        # Pick 2 sessions with neural data (one from each dir if possible)\n        sample_sessions = []\n        for dir_key in DATA_DIRS:\n            dir_sessions = [s for s in sessions if s['dir'] == dir_key]\n            if dir_sessions:\n                # Pick a medium-sized session\n                sample_sessions.append(dir_sessions[len(dir_sessions) // 2])\n            if len(sample_sessions) >= 2:\n                break\n        return sample_sessions\n\n    return sessions\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert neural data to decoder format.')\n    parser.add_argument('output', type=str, help='Output pickle file path')\n    parser.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='Process only 2 sessions')\n    parser.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='Process all sessions (default)')\n    parser.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true', help='Save processing plots')\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    # All neurons recorded from ALM\n        brain_region_idx_list.append(np.zeros(result['n_neurons'], dtype=np.int64))\n\n        session_count += 1\n\n    if session_count == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No sessions processed!\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    # Build output dictionary\n    data = {\n        'neural': all_neural,\n        'input': all_input,\n        'output': all_output,\n\n        'subjects': subjects_set,\n        'subject_idx': np.array(subject_idx_list, dtype=np.int64),\n\n        'brain_regions': ['ALM'],\n        'brain_region_idx': brain_region_idx_list,\n\n        'input_names': ['time_from_go_cue'],\n        'output_names': ['lick_direction', 'behavioral_context', 'outcome',\n                        'tongue_velocity', 'paw_velocity', 'motion_energy'],\n        'output_values': [\n            ['left', 'right', 'none'],\n            ['WC', 'DR'],\n            ['incorrect', 'correct', 'ignore'],\n            ['low', 'high', 'not_visible'],\n            ['low', 'high', 'not_visible'],\n            ['low', 'high', 'no_video'],\n        ],\n\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description': 'Delayed-response (DR) and water-cued (WC) licking task with auditory stimuli. '\n                               'Mice report left/right lick direction after sample-delay-go cue sequence.',\n            'time_bin_size': DT * 1000,  # 10 ms\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'Go cue onset (DR) / water drop (WC)',\n            'off_start': TMIN,   # -2.5s\n            'off_end': TMAX,     # 2.5s\n            'smoothing': f'Causal Gaussian, {SMOOTH_WIN} samples ({SMOOTH_WIN * DT * 1000:.0f} ms window)',\n            'quality_filter': 'all (exclude garbage, noisy)',\n            'min_firing_rate': LOW_FR_THRESH,\n            'min_units_per_session': MIN_UNITS,\n            'brain_region': 'ALM (anterior lateral motor cortex)',\n            'source_paper': 'Hasnain, Birnbaum et al., Nat Neurosci 2025',\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Print summary\n    total_neurons = sum(len(bi) for bi in brain_region_idx_list)\n    total_trials = sum(len(s) for s in all_neural)\n    n_sessions = len(all_neural)\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Conversion Summary\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Sessions: {n_sessions}\")\n    print(f\"Subjects: {len(subjects_set)}\")\n    print(f\"Total neurons: {total_neurons}\")\n    print(f\"Total trials: {total_trials}\")\n    print(f\"Neurons/session: {total_neurons/n_sessions:.1f} mean\")\n    print(f\"Trials/session: {total_trials/n_sessions:.1f} mean\")\n    print(f\"Time bins: {N_TIMEBINS} ({DT*1000:.0f} ms)\")\n    print(f\"Time window: [{TMIN}, {TMAX}] s\")\n    print(f\"Total time: {time.time() - t_start:.1f}s\")\n\n    # Save\n    print(f\"\\nSaving to {args.output}...\")\n    with open(args.output, 'wb') as f:\n        pickle.dump(data, f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)\n\n    fsize = os.path.getsize(args.output) / (1024**2)\n    print(f\"Saved ({fsize:.1f} MB)\")\n    print(\"Done!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","numLines":1173,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1173}}}

Spikes are binned at 10 ms resolution into time bins from -2.5 to 2.5 s around the go cue, producing 500 bins. The binned counts are converted to firing rates (Hz) by dividing by the bin width (0.01 s). The AI interpreted `params.dt = 1/100` as 10 ms bins (100 Hz). The reference code parameter is actually `params.dt = 1/200` (5 ms bins, 200 Hz). What processing is involved in computing `output` *Tongue velocity*?\n\ni. For each trial: (1) Extract x, y, confidence from the tracking data. (4) Interpolate velocity to the neural time bin centers using `np.interp`. This is a synthetic variable: the time axis of the binning grid, computed as the centers of the 500 time bins spanning -2.5 to 2.5 s.\n\nii.\n```python\nTIME_CENTERS = EDGES[:-1] + DT / 2\n...\ntime_input = TIME_CENTERS.astype(np.float32)\nresult['input'].append(time_input[np.newaxis, :])\n```\n\niii. Identical approach to the reference, just with different bin size (10 ms vs 5 ms).\n\n## 3-b. What processing is involved in computing `input` *Time from go cue onset in seconds*?\n\ni. No processing beyond computing bin centers from the bin edges. The time axis is defined by the binning parameters.\n\nii. N/A\n\niii. N/A\n\n## 3-c. How is `input` *Time from go cue onset in seconds* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. The time input is the center of the same bins used for neural data, so alignment is intrinsic. The same `TIME_CENTERS` array is used for both.\n\nii.\n```python\nEDGES = np.arange(TMIN, TMAX + DT, DT)\nTIME_CENTERS = EDGES[:-1] + DT / 2\n```\n\niii. Same approach as the reference.\n\n## 4-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Lick direction* derived from?\n\ni. Four per-trial fields: `bp.hit`, `bp.miss`, `bp.L`, and `bp.R`. Hit/miss indicate outcome, L/R indicate instructed side. The lick direction is inferred from the combination.\n\nii.\n```python\nif session['hit'][t]:\n    if session['L'][t]:\n        lick_dir[out_idx] = 0  # left\n    elif session['R'][t]:\n        lick_dir[out_idx] = 1  # right\nelif session['miss'][t]:\n    if session['L'][t]:\n        lick_dir[out_idx] = 1  # licked right (wrong)\n    elif session['R'][t]:\n        lick_dir[out_idx] = 0  # licked left (wrong)\n```\n\niii. The AI correctly derives lick direction from the combination of instructed side and outcome, matching the reference approach.\n\n## 4-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Lick direction*?\n\ni. A hit means the animal licked the instructed port (so lick = instructed side). A miss means the animal licked the opposite port (so lick = opposite of instructed side). Ignore trials get the default \"none\" class (2). Codes: left=0, right=1, none=2.\n\nii.\n```python\nlick_dir = np.full(n_valid, 2, dtype=np.int32)  # default: none\nfor out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n    if session['hit'][t]:\n        if session['L'][t]:\n            lick_dir[out_idx] = 0\n        elif session['R'][t]:\n            lick_dir[out_idx] = 1\n    elif session['miss'][t]:\n        if session['L'][t]:\n            lick_dir[out_idx] = 1\n        elif session['R'][t]:\n            lick_dir[out_idx] = 0\n```\n\niii. The logic matches the reference solution. The AI uses a per-trial loop instead of vectorized operations but the result is equivalent.\n\n## 5-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Behavioral context* derived from?\n\ni. The per-trial field `bp.autowater`. When autowater is 1, the trial is in the water-cued (WC) context; otherwise it is delayed-response (DR).\n\nii.\n```python\nsession['autowater'] = np.array(bp['autowater']).flatten().astype(int)\n...\nif session['autowater'][t] == 1:\n    context[out_idx] = 0\n```\n\niii. Same source variable as the reference.\n\n## 5-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Behavioral context*?\n\ni. Direct mapping: autowater=1 maps to WC (0), everything else maps to DR (1).\n\nii.\n```python\ncontext = np.full(n_valid, 1, dtype=np.int32)  # default: DR\nfor out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n    if session['autowater'][t] == 1:\n        context[out_idx] = 0\n```\n\niii. Matches the reference logic.\n\n## 6-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Outcome* derived from?\n\ni. Two per-trial fields: `bp.hit` and `bp.miss`. Neither hit nor miss implies ignore.\n\nii.\n```python\nsession['hit'] = np.array(bp['hit']).flatten().astype(bool)\nsession['miss'] = np.array(bp['miss']).flatten().astype(bool)\n```\n\niii. Same source variables as the reference.\n\n## 6-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Outcome*?\n\ni. Three classes: hit maps to correct (1), miss maps to incorrect (0), and everything else maps to ignore (2).\n\nii.\n```python\noutcome = np.full(n_valid, 2, dtype=np.int32)  # default: ignore\nfor out_idx, t in enumerate(trial_indices):\n    if session['hit'][t]:\n        outcome[out_idx] = 1\n    elif session['miss'][t]:\n        outcome[out_idx] = 0\n```\n\niii. Matches the reference logic and category codes.\n\n## 7-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Tongue velocity* derived from?\n\ni. The DLC tracking data from `obj.traj`, specifically the `tongue` feature from camera 0 (one camera only). The AI uses the `ts` array (features x [x,y,confidence] x frames), `frameTimes`, and `featNames` from a single camera view.\n\nii.\n```python\ntongue_vel, tongue_vis = compute_velocity_timeseries(\n    session, cam_idx=0, feat_name='tongue',\n    trial_indices=trial_indices, vidshift=vidshift)\n```\n\niii. The reference solution uses BOTH cameras (side camera 'tongue' and bottom camera 'top_tongue'), normalizes each by its 90th percentile, and averages them. The AI only uses one camera view.\n\n## 7-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Tongue velocity*?\n\ni. For each trial: (1) Extract x, y, confidence from the tracking data. (2) Compute velocity as `sqrt(diff(x)^2 + diff(y)^2) * 400 Hz` (raw pixel differences times frame rate). (3) Set velocity to NaN where confidence < 0.5. (4) Interpolate velocity to the neural time bin centers using `np.interp`. (5) Discretize per-session at the 50th percentile of visible values: 0=below, 1=above, 2=not visible.\n\nii.\n```python\ndx = np.diff(x)\ndy = np.diff(y)\nv = np.sqrt(dx**2 + dy**2) * VIDEO_FPS  # pixels/second\nv = np.concatenate([[0], v])\nlow_conf = conf < DLC_CONF_THRESH\nv[low_conf] = np.nan\n...\nv_interp = np.interp(taxis, aligned_ft[valid], v[valid], left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n```\n\niii. Several differences from the reference: (1) No Gaussian smoothing of x,y before differentiating (reference uses 5 ms Gaussian). (2) Uses `np.diff` instead of `np.gradient` for derivatives. (3) Multiplies by constant FPS instead of dividing by actual frame time differences. (4) Interpolates to bin centers instead of bin-averaging. (5) Confidence threshold is 0.5 instead of 0.9. (6) Does not handle contiguous runs of valid frames separately.\n\n## 7-c. How is `output` *Tongue velocity* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. The 50th percentile of all visible (confidence >= 0.5) velocity values across the session is computed. Values below the threshold get class 0, at or above get class 1, and non-visible bins get class 2.\n\nii.\n```python\ndef discretize_velocity(velocity, visible):\n    result = np.full((n_time, n_trials), 2, dtype=np.int32)\n    vis_vals = velocity[visible]\n    thresh = np.nanpercentile(vis_vals, 50)\n    result[visible & (velocity < thresh)] = 0\n    result[visible & (velocity >= thresh)] = 1\n    return result\n```\n\niii. The discretization logic matches the reference in principle (50th percentile split), but the percentile is computed only over visible values rather than over all values including NaN (the reference uses `nanpercentile` which already ignores NaN). The visibility definition differs due to the different confidence threshold (0.5 vs 0.9).\n\n## 7-d. How is `output` *Tongue velocity* aligned with the neural data?\n\ni. Frame times are corrected by subtracting the video offset (`vidshift`) and the trial's go cue time. The corrected frame times are then used to interpolate velocity values to the neural time bin centers.\n\nii.\n```python\naligned_ft = ft - vidshift - gc\n...\nv_interp = np.interp(taxis, aligned_ft[valid], v[valid], left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n```\n\niii. The video offset computation matches the reference (mode of sglx bitcode start / fs minus mode of bp bitStart). Same approach as tongue alignment.\n\n## 9-a. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Motion energy* derived from?\n\ni.
